| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF375 | \| `germany-bookkeeping` \| Transfer pricing records build on the general bookkeeping records and the related-party disclosures \| | Exact wording retained: Section 10: Interaction with Other Guides, currentLF375  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF376 | \| `de-corporate-tax` \| A correction under § 1 AStG raises taxable income and so affects corporate income tax (Körperschaftsteuer) and trade tax (Gewerbesteuer). The ministry's 2020 paper says the surcharge under § 162(4) AO cannot be deducted (no. 89) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 10: Interaction with Other Guides, currentLF376  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF377 | \| `germany-vat-return` \| The arm's length price can differ from the customs value and from the value for import VAT. Later price adjustments that raise the price must be reported to the main customs office without delay. A later credit can give a refund claim only if the adjustment was agreed in the contract, in principle and in amount, before the imports, and is tied to the products (no. 4.7 and 4.8 of the 2024 administrative principles) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 10: Interaction with Other Guides, currentLF377  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF378 | \| CbCR \| The BZSt passes each report to the competent tax office and to the treaty states, and may evaluate the reports within its legal tasks (§ 138a(7) AO). The transaction matrix is the tool the ministry names for the risk-based choice of audit fields \| | Exact wording retained: Section 10: Interaction with Other Guides, currentLF378 R61: ordinary annual deadline is qualified by the justified-expectation/no-fault one-month-from-knowledge catch-up, including specified German PE and unavailable-report notice alternative. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF379 | \| Financial statements \| Related-party disclosures under HGB or IFRS should agree with the transfer pricing position. The consolidated accounts are item 17 of the master file and the base for the revenue test in 2.4 \| | Exact wording retained: Section 10: Interaction with Other Guides, currentLF379  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF383 | 1. Find the covered dealings. List every business relation with a related person abroad within § 1(2) and (4) AStG, and every dealing between the business and a permanent establishment in another state within § 1(5) AStG. § 1 AStG is about business relations abroad. Dealings between two German related parties follow other rules (see the refer list). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF383  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF384 | 2. Set or test each price under § 1(3) AStG: function and risk analysis, comparability analysis, then the method that fits best, on the facts at the time the transaction was agreed. Where no comparable values exist, use the hypothetical arm's length test. Check the result against the range and median rules of § 1(3a) AStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF384  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF385 | 3. Test the relief for smaller businesses in § 6 GAufzV: both amounts in the relief table in 2.2, for the current financial year, with connected German businesses added. Remember the one-year lag. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__6.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF385  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF386 | 4. Build the records that § 90(3) AO asks for: the transaction matrix, the facts and the appropriateness analysis, with the content of § 4 GAufzV. Write them in German, or apply early for another language under § 2(5) GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__4.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF386  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF387 | 5. Test the master file duty under § 90(3) sentence 3 AO: part of a multinational group, and own turnover of the previous financial year against the table in 2.1. If it applies, prepare the items of the annex to § 5 GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__5.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF387  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF388 | 6. Record extraordinary transactions close in time, at the latest within six months after the end of the financial year, under § 3 GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__3.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF388  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF389 | 7. When an audit order is announced, note the date. Under § 90(4) AO the transaction matrix, the master file and the records on extraordinary transactions are due 30 days later without a request. Everything else is due 30 days after a request. Ask for a longer period early and give reasons. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF389  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF390 | 8. For groups at the revenue test in 2.4: file the country-by-country report with the BZSt under § 138a AO within one year after the financial year ends, and make the group statement in the tax return (Anlage WA for corporations). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__138a.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF390 R61: ordinary annual deadline is qualified by the justified-expectation/no-fault one-month-from-knowledge catch-up, including specified German PE and unavailable-report notice alternative. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF391 | 9. Where certainty is wanted in advance and a tax treaty applies, consider an advance pricing procedure under § 89a AO with the BZSt. Weigh the fee in Section 7 and the waiver of legal remedies. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__89a.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF391  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF392 | 10. If records were missed or are late, read § 162(3) and (4) AO before answering the tax office: late usable records and missing records lead to different surcharges, and an excusable failure leads to none. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF392  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF396 | - Who are the related parties abroad: holdings of at least one quarter, control, or a shared parent? | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF397 | - What are the total payments in the current financial year with foreign related parties, split into supplies of goods and everything else (services, interest, licences)? | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF398 | - Is the business part of a multinational group, and what was its own turnover, not the group's, in the previous financial year? | Exact wording retained: Ask the client first, currentLF436  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF399 | - What was the group's consolidated revenue in the previous financial year, who is the ultimate parent, and which company files the country-by-country report in which country? | Exact wording retained: Ask the client first, currentLF437 R61: ordinary annual deadline is qualified by the justified-expectation/no-fault one-month-from-knowledge catch-up, including specified German PE and unavailable-report notice alternative. |
